- **workflow-adsorption-energy**: Run a three-step geometry-optimization workflow in VASP for the adsorption energy of a molecule on a surface: gas-phase molecule, clean surface, and adsorbate-surface complex; the adsorption energy is obtained from total energies as E_ads=E(adsorbed)−E(molecule)−E(surface).
